2782
Researchers propose a new framework, Wasserstein-Bounded GAN (WBGAN), to improve the stability of Wasserstein GANs by adding an upper-bound constraint to the Wasserstein term, promoting faster and more stable training.
-----
-----
2305
Researchers propose using unsupervised deep language models to complete and correct search engine queries, addressing challenges through edit-distance potential and efficient CPU-based computation for real-time results.
-----
-----
2202
Researchers introduce Hierarchically Clustered Representation Learning (HCRL), a model that simultaneously optimizes representation learning and hierarchical clustering in the embedding space, enabling more meaningful cluster separations and mergers.
-----
-----
2484
Researchers proposed a method to tensorize neural networks using the Multi-scale Entanglement Renormalization Ansatz (MERA), achieving greater compression and accuracy on the CIFAR-10 dataset compared to traditional factorization methods.
-----
-----
2524
Researchers propose a new regularization scheme, MMR-Universal, for ReLU networks that provides robustness guarantees against various types of perturbations, specifically $l_1$- and $l_\infty$-perturbations, and extends to any $l_p$-norm for $p\geq 1$.
-----
-----
516
This paper proposes a residual non-local attention network for high-quality image restoration. It addresses the limitations of previous methods by incorporating local and non-local attention blocks to capture long-range dependencies and adaptively rescale hierarchical features.
-----
-----
452
Researchers used automatic search techniques to discover a new activation function, Swish (f(x) = x * sigmoid(beta * x), which outperforms the widely-used Rectified Linear Unit (ReLU) on deeper models across various challenging datasets, improving accuracy by up to 0.9% on ImageNet.
-----
-----
2325
Researchers propose two methods, Trace-norm regression (TNR) and Stable Trace-norm Analysis (StaTNA), to enhance the performance of recommender systems using side information. They outperform state-of-the-art models on MovieLens datasets.
-----
-----
2996
A novel autoencoding model is proposed, combining the strengths of variational autoencoders (VAE) and generative adversarial networks (GAN). It optimizes λ-Jeffreys divergence between model and true data distributions, achieving state-of-the-art generation and reconstruction quality.
-----
-----
2263
Researchers propose the Skip RNN model to address challenges in training Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs) on long sequences, such as slow inference and vanishing gradients, by learning to skip state updates and reducing the computational graph size.
-----
-----
2264
Researchers propose the Skip RNN model to address challenges in training Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs) on long sequences, such as slow inference and vanishing gradients, by learning to skip state updates and reducing the computational graph size.
-----
-----
1765
Researchers introduce Spectral Inference Networks, a framework for learning eigenfunctions of linear operators through stochastic optimization, which can be applied to unsupervised representation learning from video or graph-structured data.
